# Daily video prompt pack — 22/10/2026 · Snake day (ថ្ងៃឆ្នាំម្សាញ់)

Lucky signs: Snake, Ox, Rooster · Careful: Pig
Format: vertical 9:16 (YouTube Shorts, Facebook Reels, TikTok), about 45 seconds: 4 Veo scenes of about 8 seconds, then a lucky-numbers scene of about 10–12 seconds.
Two versions: make the pictures once, then record a Khmer voice-over and an English voice-over (two videos, or one video with both subtitle tracks).
Ratings, lucky signs and lucky numbers come from the site's fixed rules and match the website's daily fortune for this date.

## Luckiest animal today – Snake (ម្សាញ់) · Great Day
One hero post for the day's luckiest animal: a picture, a short video and a caption. Lucky color yellow (លឿង) · best time 01:00–03:00 · lucky numbers 9 · 92 · 987 · 9298 · 98229 (for fun only).

### Picture prompt (square 1:1)
Create a premium-quality 3D animated fantasy character of a cute, elegant golden snake with big shiny eyes and jewel-like patterned scales, the Snake of the Chinese zodiac, inspired by the charm and enchanting visual appeal of classic Disney and Pixar-style animated family films. The snake symbolizes wisdom, intuition and quiet elegance: a wise, calm and elegant expression and pose that show this. Body in creamy white-gold with shining engraved gold patterns, pink inner ears or rosy cheeks, polished gold textures, luminous highlights and delicate pink and gold accents. It sits on a round golden pedestal inside a large glowing pink lotus floating on a still pond. Behind it, a huge glowing full moon with a soft silver-gold halo, a deep indigo starry sky, a softly illuminated Khmer temple inspired by Angkor Wat with warm golden lights, palm tree silhouettes, small floating lanterns, glowing lotus flowers on the water, gentle moonbeams falling on the snake, magical sparkles and soft silver-and-gold reflections. Cinematic lighting, soft atmospheric glow, realistic metallic reflections, beautiful depth of field, professional animated-movie-quality rendering. Square 1:1, full body visible, three-quarter view, centered, with space around the character. No text, no letters, no watermark, no logo, no cropped body parts.

## 60-second video – Facebook & TikTok
A 60-second video about the Snake, today's luckiest animal: 6 clips of 10 seconds. Set the clip length in Flow to 10 seconds and add the reference pictures of Chanda and the Snake as ingredients. Chanda opens the video on camera with the hook and narrates the rest off screen; the animals never speak. For each scene, press "Copy + English voice" or "Copy + Khmer voice": it copies the style line, the scene and the voice together. Put the clips in order 1 to 6.

### Topic and audience
Today's luckiest zodiac animal, the Snake, in one minute. For viewers who follow Chinese zodiac signs, especially people born in the Year of the Snake, scrolling Facebook Reels and TikTok.

### Opening hook – 5 options
1. Identity
   English: Born in the Year of the Snake? Today is a Great Day for you. Here's your lucky color, time, and numbers.
   Khmer: អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំម្សាញ់មែនទេ? ថ្ងៃនេះ ជាថ្ងៃល្អខ្លាំងសម្រាប់អ្នក។ មកមើលពណ៌ ពេលវេលា និងលេខសំណាងរបស់អ្នក។
★ 2. Curiosity

3. Question
   English: Which zodiac animal is the luckiest today? Here's the answer, and what it means for you.
   Khmer: តើឆ្នាំណា មានសំណាងជាងគេនៅថ្ងៃនេះ? នេះគឺជាចម្លើយ និងអត្ថន័យសម្រាប់អ្នក។
4. Challenge
   English: Can you guess today's luckiest zodiac animal? Three clues: wise, intuitive, and elegant.
   Khmer: ទាយមើល៖ តើឆ្នាំណា មានសំណាងជាងគេថ្ងៃនេះ? តម្រុយ៖ ឈ្លាសវៃ ចេះវិភាគ និងសមរម្យ។
5. Fact
   English: Today is a Snake day, and in the twelve-day zodiac cycle, that makes the Snake today's luckiest sign.
   Khmer: ថ្ងៃនេះជាថ្ងៃឆ្នាំម្សាញ់ ហើយតាមវដ្ដដប់ពីរថ្ងៃ ឆ្នាំម្សាញ់ គឺជារាសីដែលមានសំណាងជាងគេថ្ងៃនេះ។
★ = used to open this video. Why: Specific and true (only the day's animal has a Great Day), creates curiosity, and every viewer wants to know if it is their sign.

### Audio design
Music: Gentle magical background music with soft chimes and a light Asian flute. No other voices: the animals do not speak. Keep it well below the voice (about 20 dB quieter) so every word is clear. Sound effects, used lightly: a soft chime when a lucky fact is revealed, a gentle whoosh on scene changes, a warm swell at the ending. No loud or scary sounds.

## 180-second video – YouTube
A 180-second video about the Snake, today's luckiest animal: 18 clips of 10 seconds. Set the clip length in Flow to 10 seconds and add the reference pictures of Chanda and the Snake as ingredients. Chanda opens the video on camera with the hook and narrates the rest off screen; the animals never speak. For each scene, press "Copy + English voice" or "Copy + Khmer voice": it copies the style line, the scene and the voice together. Put the clips in order 1 to 18.

### Topic and audience
A three-minute guide to the Snake's Great Day: meaning, character, love, work, money and lucky details. For people born in the Year of the Snake and zodiac fans on YouTube Shorts.

### Audio design
Music: Gentle magical background music with soft chimes and a light Asian flute. No other voices: the animals do not speak. Keep it well below the voice (about 20 dB quieter) so every word is clear. Sound effects, used lightly: a soft chime when a lucky fact is revealed, a gentle whoosh on scene changes, a warm swell at the ending. No loud or scary sounds.

### Scene 1 – The hook – Chanda on camera (0:00–0:10)

8

15

19

21

24

## Voice-over – story video
The same lines as the voice in each scene above, all together, for recording the voice yourself or with text-to-speech (then copy the scenes with plain "Copy", so Flow does not add a voice too).

### English
Scene 1 (0:00–0:10)
Can one little golden snake bring the light back before the full moon rises? Once upon a time, beside an ancient temple in Cambodia, our story begins.

Scene 2 (0:10–0:20)
But this morning, the moon lantern grew dim. Its light was almost gone. Without it, tonight's full-moon festival could not begin.

Scene 3 (0:20–0:30)
In the middle of the pond, a golden lotus opened, and out came a little golden snake. Today was a Snake day, and the snake felt something special.

Scene 4 (0:30–0:40)
A butterfly named Pich fluttered down. "Little Snake," she said, "only you can bring the light back. Today is your lucky day!"

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
Wise and graceful, the Snake symbolizes wisdom, intuition, and quiet elegance. Pich smiled. That was exactly why the moon had chosen it.

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
To relight the moon lantern, the little hero needed three lucky treasures: its lucky color, its lucky path, and its lucky numbers.

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
"Let's go!" said the snake, and it set off on its adventure, with Pich right by its side.

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
People born in the Year of the Snake are wise, intuitive, and elegant. Snakes are deep thinkers who plan carefully before acting.

Scene 9 (1:20–1:30)
The first treasure was waiting in a garden of a thousand lanterns. Only one shone brightest. It was yellow, the snake's lucky color today.

Scene 10 (1:30–1:40)
The snake tied a yellow ribbon around its neck. "Wear your lucky color," said Pich, "and good luck stays close to you."

Scene 11 (1:40–1:50)
Next, they came to a crossroads with many paths. One path began to glow. It pointed to the northeast, the snake's lucky direction.

Scene 12 (1:50–2:00)
But on the path stood an old, shaky bridge. The snake stopped. Sometimes, people born in the Year of the Snake can be secretive or prone to overthinking.

Scene 13 (2:00–2:10)
Pich came close and smiled. "Breathe slowly," she said. "Trust your heart. You are stronger than you think."

Scene 14 (2:10–2:20)
Step by step, the snake crossed the bridge. With every step, the planks glowed gold. On the other side, it laughed with joy.

Scene 15 (2:20–2:30)
The little hero learned that its worries were small, and its good heart was big. Balance and courage always help us grow.

Scene 16 (2:30–2:40)
At last, they reached a great stone door in the temple. Carved above it were bells, stairs, and a riddle about lucky numbers.

Scene 17 (2:40–2:50)
"The door opens only at your best time," said Pich. For the Snake today, that is from 1 to 3 in the early morning. They waited patiently.

Scene 18 (2:50–3:00)
When the time came, the snake rang the golden bells 9 times, its first lucky number. Ding! The door began to shine.

Scene 19 (3:00–3:10)
The door opened, and a stairway of light appeared. The snake climbed 92 glowing steps, its second lucky number, up to the top of the tower.

Scene 20 (3:10–3:20)
At the top of the tower, they found a shining pearl of moonlight. It was the heart of the moon lantern!

Scene 21 (3:20–3:30)
Around the pearl, seven little lights twinkled, one for each day of the week. Wednesday and Saturday shone brightest, the Snake's lucky days.

Scene 22 (3:30–3:40)
Time was running out. Holding the pearl carefully, the little hero hurried home, with Pich right beside it.

Scene 23 (3:40–3:50)
Back at the pond, everyone was waiting. The night was dark, and the full moon was rising. Was it too late?

Scene 24 (3:50–4:00)
The snake placed the pearl inside the moon lantern. For a moment, nothing happened. Then, whoosh! A golden light burst into the sky!

Scene 25 (4:00–4:10)
The lotus pond glowed again. Lanterns rose into the sky, and the full-moon festival began. Everyone cheered for the little golden hero!

Scene 26 (4:10–4:20)
The full moon shone down warmly, as if to say thank you. The snake felt peaceful and proud. It had done its very best.

Scene 27 (4:20–4:30)
If you were born in the Year of the Snake, today is your day too. Like our hero, be brave, be kind, and follow your good heart.

Scene 28 (4:30–4:40)
Let's remember today's luck. Lucky color: yellow. Best time: from 1 to 3 in the early morning. Lucky direction: northeast. Lucky numbers: 9, 92, and 987.

Scene 29 (4:40–4:50)
And so, under the bright full moon, the little golden hero and Pich fell fast asleep, dreaming of tomorrow.

Scene 30 (4:50–5:00)
That's your Chinese zodiac fortune for today. See all twelve signs and your lucky numbers at mybirthsign dot com. Follow us for tomorrow's fortune. Have a lucky day!

Scene 3 (0:20–0:30)
នៅកណ្ដាលស្រះ ផ្កាឈូកមាសមួយបានរីក ហើយមានពស់មាសតូចមួយ ចេញមក។ ថ្ងៃនេះជាថ្ងៃឆ្នាំម្សាញ់ ហើយពស់មានអារម្មណ៍ពិសេសណាស់។

Scene 4 (0:30–0:40)
មេអំបៅតូចមួយ ឈ្មោះពេជ្រ ហើរចុះមក។ ពេជ្រនិយាយថា "ពស់តូចអើយ មានតែអ្នកទេ ដែលអាចនាំពន្លឺត្រឡប់មកវិញ។ ថ្ងៃនេះ ជាថ្ងៃសំណាងរបស់អ្នក!"

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
ពស់ ឈ្លាសវៃ និងស្រស់ស្អាត។ ឆ្នាំម្សាញ់តំណាងឲ្យប្រាជ្ញា វិចារណញាណ និងភាពថ្លៃថ្នូរដ៏ស្ងប់ស្ងាត់។ ពេជ្រញញឹម។ នេះហើយ ជាហេតុដែលព្រះចន្ទ បានជ្រើសរើសវា។

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
ដើម្បីបំភ្លឺចង្កៀងព្រះចន្ទឡើងវិញ វីរបុរសតូច ត្រូវការរតនសម្បត្តិសំណាងបី៖ ពណ៌សំណាង ផ្លូវសំណាង និងលេខសំណាង។

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
ពស់និយាយថា "តោះទៅ!" ហើយវាក៏ចេញដំណើរផ្សងព្រេង ដោយមានពេជ្រ នៅក្បែរខ្លួនជានិច្ច។

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំម្សាញ់ ជាមនុស្សឈ្លាសវៃ ចេះវិភាគ និងសមរម្យ។ ម្សាញ់គិតដិតដល់មុននឹងធ្វើសកម្មភាព។

Scene 9 (1:20–1:30)
រតនសម្បត្តិទីមួយ កំពុងរង់ចាំ នៅក្នុងសួនចង្កៀងមួយពាន់។ មានតែមួយប៉ុណ្ណោះ ដែលភ្លឺជាងគេ។ វាគឺពណ៌លឿង ដែលជាពណ៌សំណាងរបស់ពស់ថ្ងៃនេះ។

Scene 10 (1:30–1:40)
ពស់ចងខ្សែបូពណ៌លឿង ជុំវិញក។ ពេជ្រនិយាយថា "ស្លៀកពាក់ពណ៌សំណាងរបស់អ្នក ហើយសំណាងល្អ នឹងនៅជិតអ្នក។"

Scene 11 (1:40–1:50)
បន្ទាប់មក ពួកគេមកដល់ផ្លូវបំបែកមួយ ដែលមានផ្លូវជាច្រើន។ ផ្លូវមួយ ចាប់ផ្ដើមភ្លឺ។ វាចង្អុលទៅទិសឦសាន ដែលជាទិសសំណាងរបស់ពស់។

Scene 12 (1:50–2:00)
ប៉ុន្តែនៅលើផ្លូវនោះ មានស្ពានចាស់មួយ ដែលរង្គើ។ ពស់ឈប់។ អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំម្សាញ់ ជួនកាលសម្ងាត់ខ្លួន ឬគិតច្រើនពេក។

Scene 13 (2:00–2:10)
ពេជ្រមកជិត ហើយញញឹម។ ពេជ្រនិយាយថា "ដកដង្ហើមយឺតៗ។ ជឿលើចិត្តរបស់អ្នក។ អ្នកខ្លាំងជាងអ្វីដែលអ្នកគិត។"

Scene 14 (2:10–2:20)
ម្ដងមួយជំហានៗ ពស់ឆ្លងកាត់ស្ពាន។ រាល់ជំហាន ក្ដារស្ពានភ្លឺពណ៌មាស។ នៅម្ខាងទៀត វាសើចយ៉ាងសប្បាយ។

Scene 15 (2:20–2:30)
វីរបុរសតូចបានរៀនថា ការព្រួយបារម្ភរបស់វា តូចទេ តែចិត្តល្អរបស់វា ធំណាស់។ តុល្យភាព និងភាពក្លាហាន តែងជួយឲ្យយើងរីកចម្រើន។

Scene 16 (2:30–2:40)
ទីបំផុត ពួកគេមកដល់ទ្វារថ្មដ៏ធំមួយ នៅក្នុងប្រាសាទ។ នៅខាងលើទ្វារ មានរូបចម្លាក់កណ្ដឹង ជណ្ដើរ និងប្រស្នាអំពីលេខសំណាង។

Scene 17 (2:40–2:50)
ពេជ្រនិយាយថា "ទ្វារនេះ បើកតែនៅពេលល្អបំផុតរបស់អ្នកប៉ុណ្ណោះ។" សម្រាប់ពស់ថ្ងៃនេះ គឺម៉ោងមួយ ដល់ម៉ោងបី ទៀបភ្លឺ។ ពួកគេរង់ចាំដោយអត់ធ្មត់។

Scene 18 (2:50–3:00)
ពេលដល់ម៉ោង ពស់វាយកណ្ដឹងមាស ចំនួនប្រាំបួន ដង ដែលជាលេខសំណាងទីមួយរបស់វា។ ទីង! ទ្វារចាប់ផ្ដើមភ្លឺ។

Scene 19 (3:00–3:10)
ទ្វារបើក ហើយមានជណ្ដើរពន្លឺមួយ លេចឡើង។ ពស់ឡើងជណ្ដើរភ្លឺ ចំនួនកៅសិបពីរ កាំ ដែលជាលេខសំណាងទីពីររបស់វា រហូតដល់កំពូលប្រាង្គ។

Scene 20 (3:10–3:20)
នៅលើកំពូលប្រាង្គ ពួកគេបានរកឃើញគុជពន្លឺព្រះចន្ទ ភ្លឺចែងចាំង។ វាគឺជាបេះដូង នៃចង្កៀងព្រះចន្ទ!

Scene 21 (3:20–3:30)
នៅជុំវិញគុជ មានពន្លឺតូចៗប្រាំពីរ ភ្លឺភ្លឹបភ្លែតៗ មួយសម្រាប់ថ្ងៃនីមួយៗក្នុងសប្ដាហ៍។ ថ្ងៃពុធ និងថ្ងៃសៅរ៍ ភ្លឺជាងគេ ដែលជាថ្ងៃសំណាងរបស់ឆ្នាំម្សាញ់។

Scene 22 (3:30–3:40)
ពេលវេលាជិតអស់ហើយ។ វីរបុរសតូច កាន់គុជយ៉ាងប្រុងប្រយ័ត្ន ហើយប្រញាប់ត្រឡប់ទៅផ្ទះវិញ ដោយមានពេជ្រ នៅក្បែរ។

Scene 23 (3:40–3:50)
នៅឯស្រះវិញ អ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា កំពុងរង់ចាំ។ យប់ងងឹត ហើយព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ កំពុងរះ។ តើយឺតពេលហើយឬ?

Scene 24 (3:50–4:00)
ពស់ដាក់គុជចូលក្នុងចង្កៀងព្រះចន្ទ។ មួយសន្ទុះ គ្មានអ្វីកើតឡើងទេ។ បន្ទាប់មក វឺត! ពន្លឺមាសមួយ បាញ់ឡើងទៅលើមេឃ!

Scene 25 (4:00–4:10)
ស្រះផ្កាឈូក ភ្លឺចែងចាំងម្ដងទៀត។ គោមហោះឡើងលើមេឃ ហើយពិធីបុណ្យព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ ក៏ចាប់ផ្ដើម។ អ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា អបអរសាទរវីរបុរសមាសតូច!

Scene 26 (4:10–4:20)
ព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ បញ្ចាំងពន្លឺកក់ក្ដៅចុះមក ហាក់ដូចជាពោលពាក្យអរគុណ។ ពស់មានអារម្មណ៍ស្ងប់ និងមានមោទនភាព។ វាបានព្យាយាមអស់ពីសមត្ថភាព។

Scene 27 (4:20–4:30)
បើអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំម្សាញ់ ថ្ងៃនេះ ក៏ជាថ្ងៃរបស់អ្នកដែរ។ ដូចវីរបុរសតូចរបស់យើង សូមក្លាហាន មានចិត្តល្អ ហើយធ្វើតាមចិត្តល្អរបស់អ្នក។

Scene 28 (4:30–4:40)
សូមចងចាំសំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ។ ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌លឿង។ ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោងមួយ ដល់ម៉ោងបី ទៀបភ្លឺ។ ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសឦសាន។ លេខសំណាង៖ លេខប្រាំបួន លេខកៅសិបពីរ និងលេខប្រាំបួនរយប៉ែតសិបប្រាំពីរ។

Scene 29 (4:40–4:50)
ហើយដូច្នេះ នៅក្រោមពន្លឺព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ វីរបុរសមាសតូច និងពេជ្រ ក៏លង់លក់ ដោយសុបិនអំពីថ្ងៃស្អែក។

Scene 30 (4:50–5:00)
នេះគឺជាជោគជតារាសីចិន សម្រាប់ថ្ងៃនេះ។ អ្នកអាចមើលរាសីទាំងដប់ពីរ និងលេខសំណាង នៅលើគេហទំព័រ mybirthsign.com។ សូមតាមដានយើង សម្រាប់ជោគជតាថ្ងៃស្អែក។ សូមឲ្យអ្នកមានសំណាងល្អ!

22
00:00:47,273 --> 00:00:49,950
នេះហើយ ជាហេតុដែលព្រះចន្ទ បានជ្រើសរើសវា។

23
00:00:50,000 --> 00:00:53,087
ដើម្បីបំភ្លឺចង្កៀងព្រះចន្ទឡើងវិញ

24
00:00:53,137 --> 00:00:56,813
វីរបុរសតូច ត្រូវការរតនសម្បត្តិសំណាងបី៖

25
00:00:56,863 --> 00:00:59,950
ពណ៌សំណាង ផ្លូវសំណាង និងលេខសំណាង។

26
00:01:00,000 --> 00:01:02,318
ពស់និយាយថា "តោះទៅ!

27
00:01:02,368 --> 00:01:05,739
" ហើយវាក៏ចេញដំណើរផ្សងព្រេង

28
00:01:05,789 --> 00:01:09,950
ដោយមានពេជ្រ នៅក្បែរខ្លួនជានិច្ច។

29
00:01:10,000 --> 00:01:13,858
អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំម្សាញ់ ជាមនុស្សឈ្លាសវៃ

30
00:01:13,908 --> 00:01:16,042
ចេះវិភាគ និងសមរម្យ។

31
00:01:16,092 --> 00:01:19,950
ម្សាញ់គិតដិតដល់មុននឹងធ្វើសកម្មភាព។

32
00:01:20,000 --> 00:01:22,264
រតនសម្បត្តិទីមួយ កំពុងរង់ចាំ

33
00:01:22,314 --> 00:01:24,248
នៅក្នុងសួនចង្កៀងមួយពាន់។

34
00:01:24,298 --> 00:01:26,727
មានតែមួយប៉ុណ្ណោះ ដែលភ្លឺជាងគេ។

35
00:01:26,777 --> 00:01:29,950
វាគឺពណ៌លឿង ដែលជាពណ៌សំណាងរបស់ពស់ថ្ងៃនេះ។

36
00:01:30,000 --> 00:01:32,499
ពស់ចងខ្សែបូពណ៌លឿង ជុំវិញក។

37
00:01:32,549 --> 00:01:36,323
ពេជ្រនិយាយថា "ស្លៀកពាក់ពណ៌សំណាងរបស់អ្នក

38
00:01:36,373 --> 00:01:38,774
ហើយសំណាងល្អ នឹងនៅជិតអ្នក។

39
00:01:38,824 --> 00:01:39,950
"

40
00:01:40,000 --> 00:01:42,845
បន្ទាប់មក ពួកគេមកដល់ផ្លូវបំបែកមួយ

41
00:01:42,895 --> 00:01:44,511
ដែលមានផ្លូវជាច្រើន។

42
00:01:44,561 --> 00:01:46,529
ផ្លូវមួយ ចាប់ផ្ដើមភ្លឺ។

43
00:01:46,579 --> 00:01:49,950
វាចង្អុលទៅទិសឦសាន ដែលជាទិសសំណាងរបស់ពស់។

44
00:01:50,000 --> 00:01:54,156
ប៉ុន្តែនៅលើផ្លូវនោះ មានស្ពានចាស់មួយ ដែលរង្គើ។

45
00:01:54,206 --> 00:01:55,277
ពស់ឈប់។

46
00:01:55,327 --> 00:01:56,959
អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំម្សាញ់

47
00:01:57,009 --> 00:01:59,950
ជួនកាលសម្ងាត់ខ្លួន ឬគិតច្រើនពេក។

48
00:02:00,000 --> 00:02:01,777
ពេជ្រមកជិត ហើយញញឹម។

49
00:02:01,827 --> 00:02:04,373
ពេជ្រនិយាយថា "ដកដង្ហើមយឺតៗ។

50
00:02:04,423 --> 00:02:06,104
ជឿលើចិត្តរបស់អ្នក។

51
00:02:06,154 --> 00:02:08,796
អ្នកខ្លាំងជាងអ្វីដែលអ្នកគិត។

52
00:02:08,846 --> 00:02:09,950
"

53
00:02:10,000 --> 00:02:13,433
ម្ដងមួយជំហានៗ ពស់ឆ្លងកាត់ស្ពាន។

54
00:02:13,483 --> 00:02:16,916
រាល់ជំហាន ក្ដារស្ពានភ្លឺពណ៌មាស។

55
00:02:16,966 --> 00:02:19,950
នៅម្ខាងទៀត វាសើចយ៉ាងសប្បាយ។

56
00:02:20,000 --> 00:02:23,371
វីរបុរសតូចបានរៀនថា ការព្រួយបារម្ភរបស់វា

57
00:02:23,421 --> 00:02:26,003
តូចទេ តែចិត្តល្អរបស់វា ធំណាស់។

58
00:02:26,053 --> 00:02:29,950
តុល្យភាព និងភាពក្លាហាន តែងជួយឲ្យយើងរីកចម្រើន។

59
00:02:30,000 --> 00:02:32,950
ទីបំផុត ពួកគេមកដល់ទ្វារថ្មដ៏ធំមួយ

60
00:02:33,000 --> 00:02:34,314
នៅក្នុងប្រាសាទ។

61
00:02:34,364 --> 00:02:37,223
នៅខាងលើទ្វារ មានរូបចម្លាក់កណ្ដឹង

62
00:02:37,273 --> 00:02:39,950
ជណ្ដើរ និងប្រស្នាអំពីលេខសំណាង។

63
00:02:40,000 --> 00:02:41,696
ពេជ្រនិយាយថា "ទ្វារនេះ

64
00:02:41,746 --> 00:02:44,474
បើកតែនៅពេលល្អបំផុតរបស់អ្នកប៉ុណ្ណោះ។

65
00:02:44,524 --> 00:02:45,982
" សម្រាប់ពស់ថ្ងៃនេះ

66
00:02:46,032 --> 00:02:48,125
គឺម៉ោង 1 ដល់ម៉ោង 3 ទៀបភ្លឺ។

67
00:02:48,175 --> 00:02:49,950
ពួកគេរង់ចាំដោយអត់ធ្មត់។

68
00:02:50,000 --> 00:02:52,746
ពេលដល់ម៉ោង ពស់វាយកណ្ដឹងមាស

69
00:02:52,796 --> 00:02:56,617
ចំនួន 9 ដង ដែលជាលេខសំណាងទីមួយរបស់វា។

70
00:02:56,667 --> 00:02:57,907
ទីង!

71
00:02:57,957 --> 00:02:59,950
ទ្វារចាប់ផ្ដើមភ្លឺ។

72
00:03:00,000 --> 00:03:03,283
ទ្វារបើក ហើយមានជណ្ដើរពន្លឺមួយ លេចឡើង។

73
00:03:03,333 --> 00:03:05,896
ពស់ឡើងជណ្ដើរភ្លឺ ចំនួន 92 កាំ

74
00:03:05,946 --> 00:03:09,950
ដែលជាលេខសំណាងទីពីររបស់វា រហូតដល់កំពូលប្រាង្គ។

75
00:03:10,000 --> 00:03:11,768
នៅលើកំពូលប្រាង្គ

76
00:03:11,818 --> 00:03:16,541
ពួកគេបានរកឃើញគុជពន្លឺព្រះចន្ទ ភ្លឺចែងចាំង។

77
00:03:16,591 --> 00:03:19,950
វាគឺជាបេះដូង នៃចង្កៀងព្រះចន្ទ!

78
00:03:20,000 --> 00:03:22,252
នៅជុំវិញគុជ មានពន្លឺតូចៗប្រាំពីរ

79
00:03:22,302 --> 00:03:23,331
ភ្លឺភ្លឹបភ្លែតៗ

80
00:03:23,381 --> 00:03:25,705
មួយសម្រាប់ថ្ងៃនីមួយៗក្នុងសប្ដាហ៍។

81
00:03:25,755 --> 00:03:27,792
ថ្ងៃពុធ និងថ្ងៃសៅរ៍ ភ្លឺជាងគេ

82
00:03:27,842 --> 00:03:29,950
ដែលជាថ្ងៃសំណាងរបស់ឆ្នាំម្សាញ់។

83
00:03:30,000 --> 00:03:31,685
ពេលវេលាជិតអស់ហើយ។

84
00:03:31,735 --> 00:03:35,256
វីរបុរសតូច កាន់គុជយ៉ាងប្រុងប្រយ័ត្ន

85
00:03:35,306 --> 00:03:39,950
ហើយប្រញាប់ត្រឡប់ទៅផ្ទះវិញ ដោយមានពេជ្រ នៅក្បែរ។

86
00:03:40,000 --> 00:03:44,485
នៅឯស្រះវិញ អ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា កំពុងរង់ចាំ។

87
00:03:44,535 --> 00:03:48,438
យប់ងងឹត ហើយព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ កំពុងរះ។

88
00:03:48,488 --> 00:03:49,950
តើយឺតពេលហើយឬ?

89
00:03:50,000 --> 00:03:53,185
ពស់ដាក់គុជចូលក្នុងចង្កៀងព្រះចន្ទ។

90
00:03:53,235 --> 00:03:55,930
មួយសន្ទុះ គ្មានអ្វីកើតឡើងទេ។

91
00:03:55,980 --> 00:03:57,303
បន្ទាប់មក វឺត!

92
00:03:57,353 --> 00:03:59,950
ពន្លឺមាសមួយ បាញ់ឡើងទៅលើមេឃ!

93
00:04:00,000 --> 00:04:02,491
ស្រះផ្កាឈូក ភ្លឺចែងចាំងម្ដងទៀត។

94
00:04:02,541 --> 00:04:03,639
គោមហោះឡើងលើមេឃ

95
00:04:03,689 --> 00:04:06,835
ហើយពិធីបុណ្យព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ ក៏ចាប់ផ្ដើម។

96
00:04:06,885 --> 00:04:09,950
អ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា អបអរសាទរវីរបុរសមាសតូច!

97
00:04:10,000 --> 00:04:13,147
ព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ បញ្ចាំងពន្លឺកក់ក្ដៅចុះមក

98
00:04:13,197 --> 00:04:15,032
ហាក់ដូចជាពោលពាក្យអរគុណ។

99
00:04:15,082 --> 00:04:17,819
ពស់មានអារម្មណ៍ស្ងប់ និងមានមោទនភាព។

100
00:04:17,869 --> 00:04:19,950
វាបានព្យាយាមអស់ពីសមត្ថភាព។

101
00:04:20,000 --> 00:04:22,323
បើអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំម្សាញ់ ថ្ងៃនេះ

102
00:04:22,373 --> 00:04:24,018
ក៏ជាថ្ងៃរបស់អ្នកដែរ។

103
00:04:24,068 --> 00:04:26,645
ដូចវីរបុរសតូចរបស់យើង សូមក្លាហាន

104
00:04:26,695 --> 00:04:29,950
មានចិត្តល្អ ហើយធ្វើតាមចិត្តល្អរបស់អ្នក។

105
00:04:30,000 --> 00:04:31,657
សូមចងចាំសំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ។

106
00:04:31,707 --> 00:04:33,039
ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌លឿង។

107
00:04:33,089 --> 00:04:35,722
ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោង 1 ដល់ម៉ោង 3 ទៀបភ្លឺ។

108
00:04:35,772 --> 00:04:37,186
ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសឦសាន។

109
00:04:37,236 --> 00:04:39,950
លេខសំណាង៖ លេខ 9 លេខ 92 និងលេខ 987។

110
00:04:40,000 --> 00:04:44,061
ហើយដូច្នេះ នៅក្រោមពន្លឺព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់

111
00:04:44,111 --> 00:04:47,506
វីរបុរសមាសតូច និងពេជ្រ ក៏លង់លក់

112
00:04:47,556 --> 00:04:49,950
ដោយសុបិនអំពីថ្ងៃស្អែក។

113
00:04:50,000 --> 00:04:52,258
នេះគឺជាជោគជតារាសីចិន សម្រាប់ថ្ងៃនេះ។

114
00:04:52,308 --> 00:04:54,373
អ្នកអាចមើលរាសីទាំង 12 និងលេខសំណាង

115
00:04:54,423 --> 00:04:56,232
នៅលើគេហទំព័រ mybirthsign.com។

116
00:04:56,282 --> 00:04:58,540
សូមតាមដានយើង សម្រាប់ជោគជតាថ្ងៃស្អែក។

117
00:04:58,590 --> 00:04:59,950
សូមឲ្យអ្នកមានសំណាងល្អ!
```

### Subtitles – English (2026-10-22-story.en.srt)
```
1
00:00:00,000 --> 00:00:02,195
Can one little golden snake bring

2
00:00:02,245 --> 00:00:05,052
the light back before the full moon rises?

3
00:00:05,102 --> 00:00:07,909
Once upon a time, beside an ancient temple

4
00:00:07,959 --> 00:00:09,950
in Cambodia, our story begins.

5
00:00:10,000 --> 00:00:11,240
But this morning

6
00:00:11,290 --> 00:00:13,337
the moon lantern grew dim.

7
00:00:13,387 --> 00:00:15,434
Its light was almost gone.

8
00:00:15,484 --> 00:00:17,934
Without it, tonight's full-moon

9
00:00:17,984 --> 00:00:19,950
festival could not begin.

10
00:00:20,000 --> 00:00:21,749
In the middle of the pond

11
00:00:21,799 --> 00:00:23,259
a golden lotus opened

12
00:00:23,309 --> 00:00:25,777
and out came a little golden snake.

13
00:00:25,827 --> 00:00:27,288
Today was a Snake day

14
00:00:27,338 --> 00:00:29,950
and the snake felt something special.

15
00:00:30,000 --> 00:00:33,065
A butterfly named Pich fluttered down.

16
00:00:33,115 --> 00:00:35,032
"Little Snake," she said

17
00:00:35,082 --> 00:00:37,901
"only you can bring the light back.

18
00:00:37,951 --> 00:00:39,950
Today is your lucky day!"

19
00:00:40,000 --> 00:00:42,905
Wise and graceful, the Snake symbolizes

20
00:00:42,955 --> 00:00:45,783
wisdom, intuition, and quiet elegance.

21
00:00:45,833 --> 00:00:46,692
Pich smiled.

22
00:00:46,742 --> 00:00:48,208
That was exactly why

23
00:00:48,258 --> 00:00:49,950
the moon had chosen it.

24
00:00:50,000 --> 00:00:52,110
To relight the moon lantern

25
00:00:52,160 --> 00:00:54,350
the little hero needed three

26
00:00:54,400 --> 00:00:56,910
lucky treasures: its lucky color

27

28
00:01:00,000 --> 00:01:03,009

29

30
00:01:06,706 --> 00:01:09,950

31
00:01:10,000 --> 00:01:12,807

32

33
00:01:15,476 --> 00:01:17,648
Snakes are deep thinkers who

34

35

36
00:01:22,239 --> 00:01:24,801

37
00:01:24,851 --> 00:01:26,666

38
00:01:26,716 --> 00:01:28,607
It was yellow, the snake's

39
00:01:28,657 --> 00:01:29,950
lucky color today.

40
00:01:30,000 --> 00:01:31,950
The snake tied a yellow

41
00:01:32,000 --> 00:01:33,950

42
00:01:34,000 --> 00:01:36,907

43
00:01:36,957 --> 00:01:39,950

44
00:01:40,000 --> 00:01:42,430

45

46

47
00:01:45,600 --> 00:01:47,710

48
00:01:47,760 --> 00:01:49,950
the snake's lucky direction.

49
00:01:50,000 --> 00:01:51,369
But on the path stood

50
00:01:51,419 --> 00:01:52,788

51
00:01:52,838 --> 00:01:54,004

52
00:01:54,054 --> 00:01:55,491

53
00:01:55,541 --> 00:01:58,261

54
00:01:58,311 --> 00:01:59,950

55

56

57

62

64

65
00:02:26,639 --> 00:02:29,950
Balance and courage always help us grow.

66
00:02:30,000 --> 00:02:32,347

67

68

69

70
00:02:40,000 --> 00:02:41,465
"The door opens only

71

72
00:02:43,788 --> 00:02:45,177

73

74
00:02:48,333 --> 00:02:49,950

75
00:02:50,000 --> 00:02:51,502

76

77
00:02:54,914 --> 00:02:56,847

78
00:02:56,897 --> 00:02:57,881

79
00:02:57,931 --> 00:02:59,950

80
00:03:00,000 --> 00:03:01,086
The door opened

81
00:03:01,136 --> 00:03:03,586

82
00:03:03,636 --> 00:03:06,162

83
00:03:06,212 --> 00:03:07,905

84
00:03:07,955 --> 00:03:09,950

85
00:03:10,000 --> 00:03:12,250
At the top of the tower

86
00:03:12,300 --> 00:03:16,250

87
00:03:16,300 --> 00:03:19,950

88
00:03:20,000 --> 00:03:22,651
Around the pearl, seven little lights

89
00:03:22,701 --> 00:03:25,497

90
00:03:25,547 --> 00:03:28,271
Wednesday and Saturday shone brightest

91
00:03:28,321 --> 00:03:29,950
the Snake's lucky days.

92
00:03:30,000 --> 00:03:32,009
Time was running out.

93
00:03:32,059 --> 00:03:34,656
Holding the pearl carefully

94
00:03:34,706 --> 00:03:37,401
the little hero hurried home

95
00:03:37,451 --> 00:03:39,950
with Pich right beside it.

96
00:03:40,000 --> 00:03:43,774
Back at the pond, everyone was waiting.

97
00:03:43,824 --> 00:03:45,538
The night was dark

98
00:03:45,588 --> 00:03:48,381
and the full moon was rising.

99
00:03:48,431 --> 00:03:49,950
Was it too late?

100
00:03:50,000 --> 00:03:51,981
The snake placed the pearl

101
00:03:52,031 --> 00:03:53,856
inside the moon lantern.

102
00:03:53,906 --> 00:03:56,278
For a moment, nothing happened.

103
00:03:56,328 --> 00:03:57,294
Then, whoosh!

104
00:03:57,344 --> 00:03:59,950
A golden light burst into the sky!

105
00:04:00,000 --> 00:04:02,104
The lotus pond glowed again.

106
00:04:02,154 --> 00:04:04,104
Lanterns rose into the sky

107
00:04:04,154 --> 00:04:06,642

108

Everyone cheered

109
00:04:07,923 --> 00:04:09,950

110
00:04:10,000 --> 00:04:12,669

111
00:04:12,719 --> 00:04:14,687

112

113
00:04:17,719 --> 00:04:19,950

If you were born in the Year of the Snake

Like our hero, be brave, be kind

117
00:04:27,787 --> 00:04:29,950

00:04:31,867 --> 00:04:33,150

00:04:34,000 --> 00:04:36,150

## Khmer voice – how to get clear Khmer
Every Khmer voice line in this pack is written for speaking: short sentences, a ។ at each pause, and numbers written out in Khmer words (for example ហុកសិបបួន, not 64) so the voice does not read them in English. Subtitles and captions keep the digits.

### If you use Google Flow
Use "Copy + Khmer voice" for each scene; the voice description is already included. Flow's Khmer can be unclear on some clips. Listen to every clip, and remake any clip where words are wrong, cut off or English-sounding. If Khmer stays unclear, make the clips without voice ("Copy") and add a recorded Khmer voice-over instead.

### If you use a text-to-speech app
- Language: Cambodian Khmer (km-KH). Pick a real Khmer voice, for example Microsoft Azure's female voice "km-KH-SreymomNeural" (used by many TTS apps and sites). Do not use an English voice with Khmer text.
- Speed: 0.85× to 0.9×. Pitch: natural. Pause between sentences: normal.
- Paste the Khmer voice-over text from this pack exactly as it is (Khmer script, no romanization).
- Make one scene at a time if the app cuts off long text, then join the clips in your editor.
- Before using a new voice, test it with these two sentences and listen carefully:
  សួស្តីអ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា! សូមស្វាគមន៍មកកាន់ MyBirthSign.com។
  ថ្ងៃនេះជាថ្ងៃល្អសម្រាប់អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំខ្លា។ សូមជឿជាក់លើខ្លួនឯង ហើយកុំភ្លេចអត់ធ្មត់។
  Check that every word is clear Cambodian Khmer, sentence endings are complete, and speed and volume stay even.

### Record it yourself
A native speaker reading the Khmer voice-over slowly and clearly is always the most natural choice.

## Style (paste first in every scene)
Vertical 9:16. Premium 3D animated fantasy film quality, inspired by classic Disney and Pixar-style animated family films: cute and majestic golden zodiac characters with oversized sparkling eyes, adorable expressive faces, soft rounded features, polished gold textures with intricate engraved ornaments and delicate pink and gold accents, alive and emotionally expressive. Setting: a softly illuminated Khmer temple inspired by Angkor Wat under a glowing full moon and a deep indigo starry sky, floating lotus flowers, ornate pink-and-gold lotus details, magical sparkles and warm golden reflections. Cinematic lighting, rich colors, soft atmospheric glow, realistic metallic reflections, beautiful depth of field, gentle slow camera movement, professional animated-movie rendering. The zodiac animals never talk and never move their mouths; only Chanda, the presenter, speaks. No text, no letters, no subtitles, no watermark, no logos.

## Scene 1 – Snake day (Chanda on camera)
Chanda, a friendly young Cambodian woman presenter with warm brown eyes, a bright natural smile, long black hair in a low bun with a small golden lotus hairpin, wearing a cream silk blouse, a deep navy sampot skirt with fine gold patterns and a small golden zodiac-wheel pendant, stands in front of the Angkor-style temple at night, facing the camera and speaking to the viewer with natural lip movements, then turns and gestures toward the pond. Chanda matches her reference image. Then: magical and powerful. A cute, elegant golden snake with big shiny eyes and jewel-like patterned scales stands on its glowing lotus pedestal on the pond in front of the Angkor-style temple under a huge full moon. Its eyes light up, a golden aura bursts around it, and it rises in a graceful spiral as glowing golden rings of light expand around it, its jewel scales shimmering. The lotus pond ripples with golden light and lanterns rise into the sky. Dynamic camera: start close on its face, then sweep up and follow it through the air. Cute, majestic and full of energy, matching the reference image.

## Scene 2 – Lucky signs
Magical and powerful. A cute, elegant golden snake with big shiny eyes and jewel-like patterned scales, a strong, adorable golden ox with big gentle eyes, polished curved horns and ruby studs on its harness and a proud, adorable golden rooster with big bright eyes, a ruby-red comb and shimmering tail feathers fly in from three directions on trails of golden light and land together on glowing lotus pedestals in front of the temple. As they land, a ring of golden energy explodes outward, lotus flowers bloom across the pond and a shower of sparkles and gold coins falls from the sky. They look at each other and cheer. The camera orbits around them in a smooth circle. The animals match the reference images.

## Scene 3 – Pig, take it easy
Premium 3D animated golden style, same characters as the reference pictures. A chubby, adorable golden piglet with big happy eyes and rosy cheeks curls up on a lotus pedestal as a soft protective bubble of golden light forms around it like a shield. Gentle glowing butterflies and petals circle it, and the full moon shines calmly on the still water. The mood is peaceful, safe and magical, not sad. The camera moves in slowly. The pig matches the reference image.

## Scene 4 – Outro
Magical and powerful. Golden light beams shoot up from the temple towers into the night sky and burst into gentle golden fireworks shaped like lotus flowers. Hundreds of floating lanterns rise over the glowing pond and the huge full moon. The camera rises up and pulls back for a wide, epic view, leaving open sky at the top of the frame for a title.

## Scene 5 – Lucky numbers (about 10–12 seconds)
Magical and powerful. A huge glowing golden zodiac wheel rises out of the lotus pond in front of the temple, with twelve cute golden zodiac animals around its rim. It spins faster and faster, shooting beams of golden light and sparkles, then slows to a gentle, shining turn under the full moon. The camera slowly pushes in, keeping open space in the middle and lower part of the frame for the number captions. (Loop the slow-turning part, or slow-zoom a still picture, to fill the time.)

## Voice in Google Flow (spoken inside each clip)
